    "EmployeeScoreEntity": [
      { name: "EmployeeScoreId", description: "Unique (System) Id", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "1", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "OST-67358" },
      { name: "EmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in the Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "EmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Employee Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "3", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeExternalId", version: "1", example: "OST-67358" },
      { name: "LocationId", description: "Location Id as specified in the location extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "4", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationId", version: "1", example: "8d695cda-f424-4652-b976-acdd833e90bf" },
      { name: "LocationExternalId", description: "Customer provided Location Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "5", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "1", example: "10034675" },

      { name: "DayOfTheYear", description: "Day of The year.", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "8", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "24" },
      { name: "RewardPoints", description: "Positive points earned on the day as specified by the scoring rules", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "117" },
      { name: "InfractionPoints", description: "Points lost on the day due to infractions as specified by the scoring rules", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "24" },
      { name: "EditedRewardPoints", description: "Positive points assigned  on the day by the manager", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "117" },
      { name: "EditedInfractionPoints", description: "Negative points assigned  on the day by the supervisor", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "117" },
      { name: "EditsReason", description: "Reasons/Notes provided by manager or supervisor for the edits made", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "Added points for exemplary work" },

      { name: "EditedDateTime", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the record was last modified", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "15", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "ApprovedByEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who approved the scores", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "16", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdExternalEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "MGR-735B2" },
      { name: "ApprovedDateTime", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the score was approved", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "17", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "Status", description: "APPROVED,OPEN", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "18", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "APPROVED" },
      { name: "RewardsReason", description: "Breakdown of the reward points calculation", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "19", formatOrSize: "Comma separated list of category:points", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "NumConfirmedMinutes:1" },

      { name: "Timestamp", description: "Timestamp of the assistant usage", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "6", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T13:00:00Z" },
      { name: "Duration", description: "Duration seconds for the assistant returns response", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "7", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Float", version: "1", example: "10" },

      { name: "Response", description: "The assistant's response", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "Here is your schedule for today..." },

      { name: "FeedbackRating", description: "The rating in user's feedback", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "harmless, not helpful" },
      { name: "FeedbackComment", description: "The user's feedback comment", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "It is a helpful response." },
      { name: "SatisfactionSentiment", description: "The user's satisfaction feedback for the whole conversation", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "true" },

      { name: "status", description: "Attribute status. Will be empty if effective dated location attribute is not enabled", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "Currently used" },
    ],
    "CommunicationInteractionEntity": [
      { name: "CommunicationInteractionId", description: "System (unique) Id", fieldType: "PrimaryKey", rank: "1", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "CommunicationId", description: "Communication Id as specified in the Communication Data Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "Type", description: "View (displayed on user’s screen), Click (user selected an image, downloaded an attachment or expanded a post), Reaction (user liked a message or post), Comment (user added a comment), Acknowledge (user selected the Acknowledge button)", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "3", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Category", version: "1", example: "View" },
      { name: "Date", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the record happened", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "4", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "Deleted", description: "Flag indicating whether the item is deleted", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "5", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "true" },
      { name: "EmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in the Employee Data Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "6", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "EmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Employee Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "7", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeExternalId", version: "1", example: "OST-67358" },
      { name: "LastModifiedDate", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the record was last modified", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "8", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who last modified the record", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdExternalEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "MGR-735B2" },
      { name: "Text", description: "The text of the comment", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "1000", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "Take a moment to relax!" },
    ],

      { name: "Year", description: "Year", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "2021" },
      { name: "DayOfTheYear", description: "Day Of The Year", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "6" },
      { name: "Deleted", description: "Flag indicating whether the entry is deleted", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "No" },
      { name: "ScheduleCost", description: "Regular Minutes (excluding unpaid meal breaks)", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "14", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
    ],
    "ConsolidatedTimesheetDataEntity": [
      { name: "Date", description: "The date of the timesheet entry", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "1", formatOrSize: "ISO (Business Timezone) Date YYYY-MM-DD", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODate", version: "1", example: "2023-08-25" },
      { name: "ParentTimesheetId", description: "An unique identifier for each timesheet", fieldType: "PrimaryKey", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "TimesheetId", description: "An unique identifier for a timesheet at a location level or work role level", fieldType: "PrimaryKey", rank: "3", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "EmployeeId", description: "An unique identifier for each employee.", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "4", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "EmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Employee Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "5", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeExternalId", version: "1", example: "OST-67358" },
      { name: "HomeLocationID", description: "An unique identifier for the employee's home location", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "6", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "HomeLocationExternalId", description: "Customer provided Location External Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "7", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "1", example: "10034675" },
      { name: "HomeLocation", description: "The name or description of the employee's home location", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "8", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationName", version: "1", example: "Front Street" },
      { name: "Home Location code", description: "A code associated with the employee's home location.", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "CostCenter", version: "1", example: "10034675" },
      { name: "WorkLocationId", description: "An unique identifier for the employee's work location", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "WorkLocationExternalId", description: "Customer provided Location Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "1", example: "10034675" },
      { name: "WorkLocation", description: "A code associated with the employee's work location", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationName", version: "1", example: "Front Street" },
      { name: "Work Location code", description: "A code associated with the employee's work location", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "CostCenter", version: "1", example: "10034675" },
      { name: "Title", description: "The job title or position of the employee", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "14", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Title", version: "1", example: "ShiftLead" },
      { name: "WorkRoleId", description: "An unique identifier for the employees work role corresponding to the hours for the date and location", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "15", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "WorkRoleId", version: "1", example: "e4d4b31c-25bc-40d2-9c45-ab44c2a46d2c" },
      { name: "WorkRole", description: "Name or description corresponding to the WorkRoleId", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "16", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "WorkRole", version: "1", example: "Key Carrier" },
      { name: "PayType", description: "ExtraAmount,ExtraTime,ExtraRateFixed,ExtraRateSupplemental,ExtraPercentage,ExtraAmountWeek,Advance", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "17", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "ExtraAmount" },
      { name: "PayName", description: "The name or description of the pay type as configured", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "18", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "Night Shift Premium" },
      { name: "PayrollEarningCode", description: "A code associated with the PayType as configured.", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "19", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "IE7963" },
      { name: "Minutes", description: "The number of minutes worked by the employee at the given location and workrole", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "20", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "8.5" },
      { name: "RateApplied", description: "The hourly or applicable rate for the employee", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "21", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "1.5" },
      { name: "Flat Rate Amount", description: "A fixed amount of payment associated with the entry", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "22", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "48.3" },
      { name: "Hourly Wage Rate", description: "The hourly wage rate for the employee based on work role or location or a combination ", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "23", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "1.5" },
      { name: "Total Wages", description: "The calculated wages for the employee based on applicable hourly rate and the pay code.", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "24", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "1.5" },
    ],

      { name: "ShiftSwapId", description: "System (unique) Id", fieldType: "PrimaryKey", rank: "1", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "LocationId", description: "Location Id as specified in the location extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationId", version: "1", example: "8d695cda-f424-4652-b976-acdd833e90bf" },

      { name: "ToEmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in the Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "f5d27549-e70b-41e8-9a09-73accc37316f" },
      { name: "ToEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Employee Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeExternalId", version: "1", example: "OST-67358" },
      { name: "OfferType", description: "ShiftSwap", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "Open" },
      { name: "OfferStatus", description: "Draft,Proposed,Claimed,Accepted,Declined,Withdrawn,Expired,Deleted", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "Draft" },
      { name: "OfferState", description: "Draft,Open,Closed", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "Draft" },
      { name: "LastModifiedDate", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the record was last modified", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "14", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "15", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "ShiftYear", description: "Year with respect to day of the year of the ToEmployee shift (requested shift). Field is 0 if ToEmployee shift is deleted.", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "16", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "2", example: "2021" },
      { name: "ShiftDayOfTheYear", description: "Day Of The Year of the ToEmployee shift (requested shift). Field is 0 if the ToEmployee shift is deleted.", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "17", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "2", example: "6" },
      { name: "HasDeletedShift", description: "Flag is true if the ToEmployee shift (requested shift) is deleted.", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "18", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "2", example: "true" },
      { name: "OfferedShiftId", description: "FromEmployee Shift Id. The offered shift id in the shift swap by FromEmployee", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "19", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "hjk16f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "OfferedShiftYear", description: "Year with respect to day of the year of the FromEmployee shift (offered shift). Field is 0 if FromEmployee shift is deleted.", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "20", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "2", example: "2021" },
      { name: "OfferedShiftDayOfTheYear", description: "Day Of The Year of the FromEmployee shift (offered shift). Field is 0 if the FromEmployee shift is deleted.", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "21", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "2", example: "6" },
      { name: "HasDeletedOfferedShift", description: "Flag is true if the FromEmployee shift (offered shift) is deleted.", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "22", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "2", example: "true" },

      { name: "CashTipAmount", description: "Cash Tip Amount", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "8.5" },
      { name: "CreditTipAmount", description: "Credit Tip Amount", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "14", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "8.5" },
      { name: "LastModifiedDate", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time. Time at which the record was last modified", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "15", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "16", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who last modified the record", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "17", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdExternalEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "MGR-735B2" },
      { name: "ParentTimesheetId", description: "Parent Timesheet Id specified in employee tip Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "18", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
    ],
    "CommunicationCommentEntity": [
      { name: "CommunicationCommentId", description: "System (unique) Id", fieldType: "PrimaryKey", rank: "1", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "CommunicationId", description: "Communication Id as specified in the Communication Data Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "Date", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the comment was sent", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "3", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "Deleted", description: "Flag indicating whether the item is deleted", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "4", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "true" },
      { name: "EmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in the Employee Data Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "5", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "EmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Employee Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "6", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeExternalId", version: "1", example: "OST-67358" },
      { name: "LastModifiedDate", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the record was last modified", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "7", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "8", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who last modified the record", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdExternalEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "MGR-735B2" },
      { name: "Text", description: "The text of the comment", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "1000", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "Take a moment to relax!" },
      { name: "LocationId", description: "Location Id as specified in the location extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationId", version: "2", example: "8d695cda-f424-4652-b976-acdd833e90bf" },
      { name: "LocationExternalId", description: "Customer provided Location Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "2", example: "10034675" },
    ],
    "TimeClockEntity": [
      { name: "TimeClockId", description: "System (unique) Id", fieldType: "PrimaryKey", rank: "1", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "ExternalClockId", description: "Customer provided Clock Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "OTC-67358" },
      { name: "EmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in the Employee Data Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "3", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "EmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Employee Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "4", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeExternalId", version: "1", example: "OST-67358" },
      { name: "LocationId", description: "Location Id as specified in the Location Data Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "5", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "LocationExternalId", description: "Customer provided Location Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "6", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "1", example: "10034675" },
      { name: "WorkRoleId", description: "System (unique) id", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "7", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "e4d4b31c-25bc-40d2-9c45-ab44c2a46d2c" },
      { name: "WorkRole", description: "Customer provided work role name", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "8", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "Key Carrier" },
      { name: "LastModifiedDate", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the record was last modified", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in the Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who last modified the record", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdExternalEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "MGR-735B2" },
      { name: "ApprovedBy", description: "Employee Id as specified in the Employee Data Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "05569cf5-5041-4998-9a7f-8b9e706b211d" },
      { name: "ApprovedByEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who approved the clock", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeExternalId", version: "1", example: "MGR-735B2" },
      { name: "Deleted", description: "Flag indicating whether the clock is deleted", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "14", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "No" },
      { name: "InitiatorEmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in Employee Extract", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "15", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "InitiatorEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who initiated the clock", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "16", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeExternalId", version: "1", example: "OST-67358" },
      { name: "Status", description: "Created,Approved,Rejected,Invalid,PendingApproval,Duplicate", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "17", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "Created" },
      { name: "TimesheetId", description: "System Id of the associated timesheet", fieldType: "ForeignKey", rank: "18", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "6eb13e0c-60e4-44d2-a158-a73d5d49ddb3" },
      { name: "ShiftId", description: "System id of the associated shift", fieldType: "ForeignKey", rank: "19", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "6d50520c-4071-47b8-a248-892c24cdd603" },
      { name: "WaiverType", description: "NotApplicable,Waived,NotWaived", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "20", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "NotApplicable" },
      { name: "OriginatingSystem", description: "KIOSK,SQUARE,CONSOLEUI,OTHER_INTEGRATION,AUTOMATIC_GENERATION,TMMobile,KIOSK_BIOMETRIC", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "21", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "KIOSK" },
      { name: "ApprovedOn", description: "ISO (UTC) datetime at which the clock got approved", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "22", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "CreatedDate", description: "ISO (UTC) datetime at which the clock got created", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "23", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-01T00:30:21Z" },
      { name: "ClockTime", description: "Adjusted ISO (UTC) datetime of the clock", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "24", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T09:00:00Z" },
      { name: "OriginalClockTime", description: "Original ISO (UTC) datetime of the clock", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "25", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T09:00:00Z" },
      { name: "ClockType", description: "ShiftBegin,MealBreak,MealBreakBegin,MealBreakEnd,RestBreak,RestBreakBegin,RestBreakEnd,ShiftEnd,TransitionOut,TransitionIn", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "26", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "ShiftBegin" },
      { name: "ManagerOverride", description: "Flag indicating whether the clock was overridden by the manager", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "27", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "Note", description: "Note", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "28", formatOrSize: "500", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "Add Clock Before" },
      { name: "ParentTimesheetId", description: "System Id of the parent timesheet", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "29", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "6eb13e0c-60e4-44d2-a158-a73d5d49ddb3" },
      { name: "ParentTimesheetYear", description: "Year of the parent timesheet", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "30", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "2025" },
      { name: "ParentTimesheetDayOfTheYear", description: "Day of the Year of the parent timesheet", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "31", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "114" },
      { name: "ClockTimeMinutes", description: "ClockTime represented as minutes since midnight of the parent timesheet date (0 - 2880).Seconds are included as fractional minutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "32", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "2", example: "1340.547" },
    ],
    "LaborBudgetEntity": [
      { name: "LaborBudgetId", description: "System (unique) Id", fieldType: "PrimaryKey", rank: "1", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a33" },
      { name: "LaborBudgetName", description: "Name", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "FY2022 budget" },
      { name: "LaborBudgetDescription", description: "Description", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "3", formatOrSize: "4096", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "FY2022 budget" },
      { name: "LaborBudgetStatus", description: "In Progress,Ready for Review,Reviewed - Approved,Reviewed - Rejected,In Effect", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "4", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "InProgress" },
      { name: "DurationStartDate", description: "Budget plan start date", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "5", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODate", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "DurationEndDate", description: "Budget plan end date", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "6", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODate", version: "1", example: "2022-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "LocationId", description: "Location Id as specified in the location extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "7", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationId", version: "1", example: "8d695cda-f424-4652-b976-acdd833e90bf" },
      { name: "LocationExternalId", description: "Customer provided Location Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "8", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "1", example: "10034675" },
      { name: "LastModifiedDate", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the record was last modified", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who last modified the record", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdExternalEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "MGR-735B2" },
    ],
    "EmployeeActivityEntity": [
      { name: "EmployeeActivityId", description: "Unique (System) Id", fieldType: "PrimaryKey", rank: "1", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "2", example: "de2429cc-0f0a-4614-9d58-9d1e6d1eec51-2025067" },
      { name: "EmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in the Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "EmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Employee Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "3", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeExternalId", version: "1", example: "OST-67358" },
      { name: "LocationId", description: "The home location of the employee. This is Legion generated universally unique ID.", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "4", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationId", version: "1", example: "8d695cda-f424-4652-b976-acdd833e90bf" },
      { name: "LocationExternalId", description: "The home location of the employee. This is customer provided Location Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "5", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "1", example: "10034675" },
      { name: "Year", description: "Year", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "6", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "2021" },
      { name: "DayOfTheYear", description: "Day of The year.", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "7", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "24" },
      { name: "ConfirmedAvailableMinutes", description: "Duration in minutes;  Indicates time duration within the operating hours when the employee is available for the day used for shift assignment purposes, as configured by the employee", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "8", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "UnspecifiedAvailableMinutes", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates time duration within the operating hours when the employee is considered available for shift assignment purposes, when none configured exclusively.", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "UnavailableMinutes", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates time duration within the operating hours when the employee is not available for shift assignment purposes, as configured by the employee.", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "TimeOffMinutes", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates time duration within the operating hours when the employee has requested for time off", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "ScheduledMinutesHomeLocation", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates time duration of shift assigned to the employee for the day at the home location of employee ", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "ScheduledMinutesOtherLocations", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates time duration of shift assigned to the employee for the day at a location other than the employee's home location ", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "VolunteersForMoreHours", description: "Indicates if employee has opted to volunteer for additional work. Value Yes indicates that the employee has opted for more hours and value No indicates that the employee has not opted for more hours.", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "14", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "1" },
      { name: "NumLocationsSubscribedForOffers", description: "Number of locations in the vicinity of the employee's home location where the employee has opted to work", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "15", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "3" },
      { name: "NumCoverShiftOffersAccepted", description: "Number of offers that the employee accepted for the day volunteering to cover for a colleague", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "16", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "NumOpenShiftOffersAccepted", description: "Number of open shift offers that the employee accepted for the day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "17", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "NumSwapShiftOffersAccepted", description: "Number of swap shift offers that the employee accepted for the day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "18", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "TotalOffersAccepted", description: "Total number of open shift, swap or cover shift offers the employee accepted for the day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "19", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "NumShiftsDropInitiated", description: "Number of employee initiated drop/cover shift requests for the day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "20", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "NumShiftsSwapsInitiated", description: "Number of employee initiated swap shift requests for the day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "21", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "TotalShiftOffersInitiated", description: "Total number of shift swaps and shift drop requests initiated by the employee for the day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "22", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "EmployeeHasAccessedLegion", description: "Number of times employee has accessed Legion for the day.", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "23", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "12" },
      { name: "ClockedMinutesHomeLocation", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates the time duration clocked by the employee at their home location", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "24", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "ClockedMinutesOtherLocations", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates the time duration clocked by the employee other locations", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "25", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "EarlyClockInMinutes", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates the amount of time by which the employee was early to the scheduled shift on this day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "26", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "EarlyClockOutMinutes", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates the amount of time by which the employee clocked out earlier than  the scheduled end of shift", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "27", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "LateClockInMinutes", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates the amount of time by which the employee clocked in later than  the scheduled start of shift", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "28", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "LateClockOutMinutes", description: "Duration in minutes; Indicates the amount of time by which the employee clocked out later than  the scheduled end of shift", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "29", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "NumberOfNoShow", description: "This will count any no show that the employee accrues, whether it is an Absence or No Call / No Show.", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "30", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "ScheduleAvailabilityOverlapMinutes", description: "Duration in minutes; Overlap minutes between schedule and availability ", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "31", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "330" },
      { name: "EngagementId", description: "Engagement ID", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "32", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "679fad7b-2421-4fab-ac37-d5375014440e" },
      { name: "NumberOfAbsences", description: "Number of absences that have been recorded in the timesheet with the Absence option set.", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "33", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "3", example: "120" },
      { name: "NumberOfNoCallNoShow", description: "Number of No Call / No Show that have been recorded in the timesheet with the No Call / No Show option set.", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "34", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "3", example: "75" },
      { name: "NewsfeedPosts", description: "Number of newsfeed posts created by the employee on this day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "35", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "4", example: "5" },
      { name: "NewsfeedComments", description: "Number of newsfeed comments made by the employee on this day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "36", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "4", example: "3" },
      { name: "NewsfeedReactions", description: "Number of newsfeed reactions by the employee on this day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "37", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "4", example: "8" },
      { name: "NewsfeedReads", description: "Number of newsfeed posts read by the employee on this day", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "38", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "4", example: "12" },

      { name: "Engaged", description: "Flag indicating whether the employee has started using the system", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "15", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "Yes" },

    "OtherPayEntity": [
      { name: "OtherPayId", description: "System (unique) Id of the record", fieldType: "PrimaryKey", rank: "1", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "Deleted", description: "Flag indicating whether this other pay is deleted.", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "TimesheetId", description: "Timesheet Id referred to by the Timesheet Entity extract", fieldType: "PrimaryKey", rank: "3", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "PrimaryKeyId", version: "1", example: "b2916f25-20bf-4554-9be5-1937d68a3a31" },
      { name: "EmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in the Employee Data Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "4", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "EmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Employee Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "5", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeExternalId", version: "1", example: "OST-67358" },
      { name: "LocationId", description: "Location Id as specified in the Location Data Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "6", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "LocationExternalId", description: "Customer provided Location Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "7", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "1", example: "10034675" },
      { name: "Year", description: "Year", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "8", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "2021" },
      { name: "DayOfTheYear", description: "Day of The year", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "24" },
      { name: "LastModifiedDate", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the record was last modified", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who last modified the record", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdExternalEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "MGR-735B2" },
      { name: "PenaltyType", description: "Time,Currency,TimeProportional,TimeLimited,None,TimeRange,ShiftLength", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "Time" },
      { name: "Name", description: "Name of the other pay provided by the customer", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "14", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "Custom Extra Pay" },
      { name: "Value", description: "Minutes or Amount", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "15", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Double", version: "1", example: "23.45" },
    ],

      { name: "EmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in the Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "EmployeeId", version: "1", example: "f5d27549-e70b-41e8-9a09-73accc37316f" },

      { name: "NoDayDT", description: "Flag is true for no daily double-time overtime and is derived from Schedule Agreement Policy", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "18", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "true" },
      { name: "DayDTMinutes", description: "Daily double-time minutes derived from Schedule Agreement Policy", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "19", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "10" },
      { name: "NoWeekDT", description: "Flag is true for no weekly double-time overtime and is derived from Schedule Agreement Policy", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "20", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "true" },
      { name: "WeekDTMinutes", description: "Weekly double-time minutes derived from Schedule Agreement Policy", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "21", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "10" },
      { name: "EnforcesNumberOfShifts", description: "Flag is true if schedule agreement policy name is 4 x 10 or 5 x 8", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "22", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "true" },
      { name: "EnforcesShiftLength", description: "Flag is true if schedule agreement policy name is 4 x 10 or 5 x 8", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "23", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "true" },
      { name: "ShiftMinLength", description: "The minimum length a worker's shift can be in hours", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "24", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Float", version: "1", example: "10" },
      { name: "ShiftMaxLength", description: "The maximum length a worker's shift can be in hours", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "25", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Float", version: "1", example: "10" },
      { name: "NumberOfHoursPerWeekMinimum", description: "The minimum number of hours a worker prefers per week", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "26", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "10" },
      { name: "NumberOfHoursPerWeek", description: "The number of hours a worker prefers per week", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "27", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "10" },
      { name: "NumberOfShiftsPerWeekMinimum", description: "The minimum number of shifts a worker prefers per week", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "28", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "10" },
      { name: "NumberOfShiftsPerWeek", description: "The number of shifts a worker prefers per week", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "29", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "10" },
      { name: "OtherLocationsPreference", description: "Flag is true if worker has a preference working at other locations", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "30", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "true" },
      { name: "VolunteerMoreHours", description: "Flag is true if worker is on voluntary standby", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "31", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "true" },
      { name: "ValidFrom", description: "The start date from which the worker preferences are valid.", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "32", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2025-02-10T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "ValidTo", description: "The end date from which the worker schedule preferences are valid.", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "33", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2025-02-12T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "MorningShiftsMax", description: "Maximum number of morning shifts per time period", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "34", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "2", example: "2" },
      { name: "EveningShiftsMax", description: "Maximum number of evening shifts per time period", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "35", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "2", example: "3" },
      { name: "WeekendShiftsMax", description: "Maximum number of weekend shifts per time period", fieldType: "Measure", rank: "36", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "2", example: "2" },

      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who last modified the record", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdExternalEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "MGR-735B2" },
      { name: "IsEarlyClockInAlerts", description: "Flag is true if the timesheet has an early clock in", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "IsLateClockInAlerts", description: "Flag is true if the timesheet has a late clock in", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "IsUnscheduleClockIn", description: "Flag is true if the timesheet has an unscheduled clock in", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "IsIncompleteClockIn", description: "Flag is true if the timesheet has an incomplete clock in", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "HasCurrentIncompleteClockIn", description: "Flag is true if the timesheet has a currently incomplete clock in", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "14", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "HasOverlappingClocksAndTimeOffs", description: "Flag is true if timesheet has overlapping clocks and time offs", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "15", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "IsNoShowWithClockInAlerts", description: "Flag is true if the timesheet has a no show with a clock in", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "16", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "IsMissClockInAlerts", description: "Flag is true if the timesheet has a missed clock in", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "17", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "IsMissMealBreakAlerts", description: "Flag is true if the timesheet has a missed meal break", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "18", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "Yes" },
      { name: "MealAndRestPremiumMins", description: "Meal and Rest Premium Minutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "19", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "1", example: "30" },

      { name: "Approvable", description: "Flag is true if the timesheet is approvable. Value can be null for pending approval.", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "21", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "1", example: "true" },
      { name: "UnapprovableReason", description: "The reason why a timesheet is unapprovable and is optionally provided", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "22", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "IS_INCOMPLETE_CLOCK_IN" },

      { name: "IsMissRestBreakAlerts", description: "Flag is true if the timesheet has a missed rest break", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "25", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "YesNo", version: "1", example: "Yes" },

      { name: "LocationExternalId", description: "Customer provided Location Id", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "2", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "1", example: "10034675" },
      { name: "LocationName", description: "Customer provided Location Name", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "3", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationName", version: "1", example: "Front Street" },
      { name: "LastModifiedDate", description: "ISO (UTC) Date Time.  Time at which the record was last modified", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "4", formatOrSize: "ISO UTC Date YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ssZ", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODateTime", version: "1", example: "2021-01-06T08:30:21Z" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeId", description: "Employee Id as specified in Employee Extract", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "5", formatOrSize: "36", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "27d5f23e-7525-45ec-81da-48f4e6fc247b" },
      { name: "LastModifiedByEmployeeExternalId", description: "Customer provided Id of the employee who last modified the record", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "6", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "UserIdExternalEmployeeId", version: "1", example: "MGR-735B2" },
      { name: "Status", description: "CREATED,ENABLED,DISABLED,PROGRESS,DELETED", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "7", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "CREATED" },
      { name: "Contact", description: "Contact", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "8", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1" },
      { name: "Address", description: "Location Address", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "9", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "252 Front Street, San Francisco, CA 96593" },
      { name: "TimeZone", description: "Time zone of the location", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "10", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "1", example: "America/Los_Angeles" },
      { name: "StartOfWeek", description: "Sunday,Monday,Tuesday,Wednesday,Thursday,Friday,Saturday", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "11", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "Sunday" },
      { name: "LocationType", description: "Real,LocationGroup,Test,HQ,District,UpperField", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "12", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "Real" },
      { name: "LocationSubType", description: "Mock,NSO,Real", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "13", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "1", example: "Mock" },
      { name: "ParentLocationId", description: "Parent Location Id", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "14", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "1", example: "DIST-34562" },
      { name: "EffectiveDate", description: "ISO Date at which the location went live", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "15", formatOrSize: "ISO (Business Timezone) Date YYYY-MM-DD", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODate", version: "1", example: "2020-01-03" },
      { name: "CostCenter", description: "Cost Center Code", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "16", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "1126" },
      { name: "LocationGroupType", description: "FlexGroup,MasterSlave,PeerToPeer,None", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "17", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Enum", version: "3", example: "FlexGroup" },
      { name: "Level", description: "Location level in the hierarchy", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "18", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "3", example: "1" },
      { name: "ReadyForForecast", description: "Whether the location is ready for forecast", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "19", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Boolean", version: "3", example: "true" },
      { name: "ConfigType", description: "Configuration type of the location", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "20", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "3" },
      { name: "LaunchDate", description: "ISO Date at which the NSO location was launched", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "21", formatOrSize: "ISO (Business Timezone) Date YYYY-MM-DD", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODate", version: "3", example: "2020-01-03" },
      { name: "EndComparableStoreDate", description: "ISO Date at which the NSO comparable store period ends", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "22", formatOrSize: "ISO (Business Timezone) Date YYYY-MM-DD", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "ISODate", version: "3", example: "2021-01-03" },
      { name: "ComparableLocation", description: "External Id of the comparable (tracked source) location", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "23", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "LocationExternalId", version: "3", example: "10034675" },

      { name: "ThirdMealBreakCoverageId", description: "ID of Shift Covering Third Meal Break", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "49", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "19846830" },
      { name: "FourthMealBreakCoverageId", description: "ID of Shift Covering Fourth Meal Break", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "50", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "19846830" },
      { name: "FifthMealBreakCoverageId", description: "ID of Shift Covering Fifth Meal Break", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "51", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "19846830" },
      { name: "FirstRestBreakCoverageId", description: "ID of Shift Covering First Rest Break", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "52", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "19846830" },
      { name: "SecondRestBreakCoverageId", description: "ID of Shift Covering Second Rest Break", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "53", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "19846830" },
      { name: "ThirdRestBreakCoverageId", description: "ID of Shift Covering Third Rest Break", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "54", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "19846830" },
      { name: "FourthRestBreakCoverageId", description: "ID of Shift Covering Fourth Rest Break", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "55", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "19846830" },
      { name: "FifthRestBreakCoverageId", description: "ID of Shift Covering Fifth Rest Break", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "56", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "2", example: "19846830" },
      { name: "ParentTimesheetId", description: "The associated parent timesheet id for a shift. Value is null if no parent timesheet exists or if the shift is an open shift.", fieldType: "Dimension", rank: "57", formatOrSize: "255", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "String", version: "3", example: "195ced09-c60f-4ee1-bbad-92109a4fe92a" },
      { name: "FirstMealBreakPaidMinutes", description: "FirstMealBreakPaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "58", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FirstMealBreakUnpaidMinutes", description: "FirstMealBreakUnpaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "59", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "SecondMealBreakPaidMinutes", description: "SecondMealBreakPaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "60", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "SecondMealBreakUnpaidMinutes", description: "SecondMealBreakUnpaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "61", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "ThirdMealBreakPaidMinutes", description: "ThirdMealBreakPaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "62", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "ThirdMealBreakUnpaidMinutes", description: "ThirdMealBreakUnpaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "63", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FourthMealBreakPaidMinutes", description: "FourthMealBreakPaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "64", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FourthMealBreakUnpaidMinutes", description: "FourthMealBreakUnpaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "65", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FifthMealBreakPaidMinutes", description: "FifthMealBreakPaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "66", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FifthMealBreakUnpaidMinutes", description: "FifthMealBreakUnpaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "67", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FirstRestBreakPaidMinutes", description: "FirstRestBreakPaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "68", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FirstRestBreakUnpaidMinutes", description: "FirstRestBreakUnpaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "69", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "SecondRestBreakPaidMinutes", description: "SecondRestBreakPaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "70", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "SecondRestBreakUnpaidMinutes", description: "SecondRestBreakUnpaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "71", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "ThirdRestBreakPaidMinutes", description: "ThirdRestBreakPaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "72", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "ThirdRestBreakUnpaidMinutes", description: "ThirdRestBreakUnpaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "73", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FourthRestBreakPaidMinutes", description: "FourthRestBreakPaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "74", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FourthRestBreakUnpaidMinutes", description: "FourthRestBreakUnpaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "75", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FifthRestBreakPaidMinutes", description: "FifthRestBreakPaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "76", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
      { name: "FifthRestBreakUnpaidMinutes", description: "FifthRestBreakUnpaidMinutes", fieldType: "Attribute", rank: "77", formatOrSize: "n/a", encoding: "UTF-8", columnType: "Integer", version: "4", example: "30" },
    ],
  };

  const downloadCSV = () => {
    const headers = ['Name', 'Description', 'Field Type', 'Rank', 'Format/Size', 'Encoding', 'Column Type', 'Version', 'Example'];
    const rows = dataDictionary[selected]?.map(row => [
      row.name,
      row.description,
      row.fieldType,
      row.rank,
      row.formatOrSize,
      row.encoding,
      row.columnType,
      row.version,
      row.example
    ]) || [];

    const csvRows = [headers, ...rows].map(row =>
      row.map(cell => `"${String(cell).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(',')
    );

    const csvContent = csvRows.join('\r\n');

    const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    link.setAttribute('download', `${selected.replace(/\s+/g, '_')}_DataDictionary.csv`);
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
  };

  const filteredExtracts = extracts.filter(ext =>
    ext.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '2rem' }}>
      <div style={{ width: '350px', borderRight: '1px solid #eee', paddingRight: '1rem' }}>
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Search extracts..."
          style={{ width: '100%', padding: '0.5rem', marginBottom: '1rem' }}
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
        />
        <ul style={{ listStyle: 'none', paddingLeft: 0 }}>
          {filteredExtracts.map(extract => (
            <li key={extract} style={{ marginBottom: '0.5rem', cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: extract === selected ? 'bold' : 'normal' }} onClick={() => setSelected(extract)}>
              {extract}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div style={{ flexGrow: 1 }}>
        <h2>{selected ? `${selected} Data Dictionary` : 'Data Dictionary'}</h2>

        {selected === '' ? (
          <div style={{
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            height: '300px',
            maxWidth: '500px',
            margin: '3rem auto',
            background: '#f8f9fa',
            borderRadius: '12px',
            boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.07)',
            padding: '2rem',
            textAlign: 'center',
          }}>
            <h3 style={{ marginBottom: '1rem', color: '#007bff' }}>Welcome to the Legion Data Extracts Data Dictionary</h3>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '1.1rem', color: '#333' }}>
              Select an extract from the list to view its data dictionary.<br />
              Use the search box to quickly find an extract.
            </p>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '0.98rem', color: '#555', marginTop: '2rem' }}>
              Please refer to <a href="https://developers.legion.co/page/legion-data-model-overview#/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{ color: '#007bff', textDecoration: 'underline' }}>Legion Data Model Overview</a> for the Legion Data Model fundamentals.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : dataDictionary[selected] ? (
          <>
            <button
              onClick={downloadCSV}
              style={{
                marginTop: '1rem',
                marginBottom: '1rem',
                padding: '0.5rem 1rem',
                backgroundColor: '#007bff',
                color: '#fff',
                border: 'none',
                borderRadius: '4px',
                cursor: 'pointer'
              }}
            >
              ⬇️ Download CSV
            </button>

            <div style={{
              overflowX: 'auto',
              width: '100%',
              maxWidth: '100%'
            }}>
              <table style={{
                width: '1200px',
                borderCollapse: 'collapse',
                tableLayout: 'fixed',
                minWidth: '1200px'
              }}>
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th style={{ textAlign: 'left', borderBottom: '1px solid #ccc', padding: '0.5rem', width: '15%' }}>Name</th>
                    <th style={{ textAlign: 'left', borderBottom: '1px solid #ccc', padding: '0.5rem', width: '25%' }}>Description</th>
                    <th style={{ textAlign: 'left', borderBottom: '1px solid #ccc', padding: '0.5rem', width: '10%' }}>Field Type</th>
                    <th style={{ textAlign: 'left', borderBottom: '1px solid #ccc', padding: '0.5rem', width: '5%' }}>Rank</th>
                    <th style={{ textAlign: 'left', borderBottom: '1px solid #ccc', padding: '0.5rem', width: '10%' }}>Format/Size</th>
                    <th style={{ textAlign: 'left', borderBottom: '1px solid #ccc', padding: '0.5rem', width: '10%' }}>Encoding</th>
                    <th style={{ textAlign: 'left', borderBottom: '1px solid #ccc', padding: '0.5rem', width: '10%' }}>Column Type</th>
                    <th style={{ textAlign: 'left', borderBottom: '1px solid #ccc', padding: '0.5rem', width: '5%' }}>Version</th>
                    <th style={{ textAlign: 'left', borderBottom: '1px solid #ccc', padding: '0.5rem', width: '10%' }}>Example</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {dataDictionary[selected].map((field, index) => (
                    <tr key={index}>
                      <td style={{ padding: '0.5rem', borderBottom: '1px solid #eee', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}>{field.name}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '0.5rem', borderBottom: '1px solid #eee', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}>{field.description}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '0.5rem', borderBottom: '1px solid #eee', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}>{field.fieldType}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '0.5rem', borderBottom: '1px solid #eee', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}>{field.rank}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '0.5rem', borderBottom: '1px solid #eee', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}>{field.formatOrSize}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '0.5rem', borderBottom: '1px solid #eee', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}>{field.encoding}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '0.5rem', borderBottom: '1px solid #eee', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}>{field.columnType}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '0.5rem', borderBottom: '1px solid #eee', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}>{field.version}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '0.5rem', borderBottom: '1px solid #eee', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}>{field.example}</td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <p style={{ marginTop: '1rem' }}>Data dictionary not available.</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
